पदार्थ पत्र प्राकृतिक गैस लाने के लिये नारनगम फैिन केमि मनिनन्लार । पाइप लाइन परियोजना के अन्तर्गत पाइप लाइन गैस 
अथारिटी ऑफ इण्डिया लिमिटेड द्वारा बिछाया जाना है ।

अधिकार ग्रहण करना आवश्यक है ।

का . प्रा . 6381 जबकि केन्द्र सरकार यह अनुभव करती है कि सार्वजनिक हित में यह आवश्यक है कि पैट्रोलियम पदार्थ 
एवं प्राकृतिक गैस लाने के लिए एच . बी . जे . पाशलाइन परियोजना के अन्तर्गत एक पाइप लाइन गैस अथारिटी आफ इंडिया लिमिटेड 
द्वारा बिछाई जानी है । 

और यह भी अनुभव करती है कि उस कार्य के लिए इसके साथ संलग्न विवरणी में निर्धारित भूमि पर प्रयोक्ता का अधिकार 
ग्रहण करना आवश्यक है । 

अतः पैट्रोलियम एवं खनिज पाइप लाइन ( भूमि पर प्रयोक्ता का अधिकार ग्रहण अधिनियम, 1962 ( 1962 का 50 ) के 
खंड 3 के उपखंड ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्र सरकार एतदद्वारा उस पर प्रयोक्ता के अधिकार ग्रहण करने 
की मंशा की घोषणा करती है । 

बशर्ते कि उक्त भूमि में अपनी रुचि रखने वाले कोई भी व्यक्ति अधिसूचना की तारीख से 21 दिन के भीतर भूमिगत 
पाइपलाइन बिछाने के विरोध में अपनी आपत्ति सक्षम प्राधिकारी , गैस अथारिटी माफ इंडिया लि . , एच , बी . जे . पाइप लाइन परि 
योजना, विकासदीप बिल्डिंग, 22 स्टेशन रोड , लखनऊ - 2260 19, उ . प्र . में दर्ज करा सकता है । 


और ऐसी आपत्ति दर्ज कराते समय किसी भी व्यक्ति को यह विशेष रूप से निर्दिष्ट करना होगा कि वह व्यक्तिगत रूप से 
अथवा विधि व्यवसायिक के माध्यम से अपना मत प्रस्तुत करना चाहता है ।

का . प्रा . 639 - - यतः पेट्रोलियम और खनिज पाइप लाइन ( भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का अर्जन ) अधिनियम 1962. 
( 1962 का 50 ) की धारा 3 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन भारत सरकार के पेट्रोलियम एवं प्राकृतिक गैस अधिसूचना का . प्रा . 
सं . 2072 तारीख 3- 8-92 द्वारा केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उस अधिसूचना से संलग्न अनुमूची में विनिर्दिष्ट भमियों के उपयोग के अधिकार 
को पाइप लाइनों को बिछाने के लिए अजित करने का अपना प्राशय घोषित कर दिया था । 

और यतः सक्षम प्राधिकारी ने उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन सरकार को रिपोर्ट दे दी है । 

और भागे यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उक्त रिपोर्ट पर विचार करने के पश्चात इस अधिसूचना से संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट 
भूमियों में उपयोग का अधिकार अर्जित करने का विनिश्चय किया है । 

अब अतः उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्ति का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार एतद्वारा 
घोषित करती है कि इस अधिसूचना में संलग्न अनुसूची विनिर्दिष्ट उक्त भूमियों में उपयोग का अधिकार पाइप लाइन बिछाने के प्रयोजन 
के लिए एतद द्वारा अर्जित किया जाता है । 

और आगे उस धारा की उपधारा ( 4 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार निर्देश देती है कि उक्त 
भमियों में उपयोग का अधिकार केन्द्रीय सरकार में निहित होने के बजाय भारतीय गैस प्राधिकरण लि . में सभी बाधामों से महा 
रूप में घोषणा के प्रकाशन की इस तारीख को निहित होगा ।

प्रातः पंद्रोलियम एवं खनिज पाटप लाइन ( भमि पर प्रयोक्ता का अधिकार ग्रहण ) अधिनियम , 1962 ( 1962 का 50 ) के 
खंड 3 के उपखंड ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए मरकार एतदद्वारा उस पर प्रयोक्ता के अधिकार ग्रहण करने की 
मंशा की घोषणा करती है ।

विकासदीप बिल्डिंग , 22 स्टेशन रोड , लखनऊ - 226019 उ . प्र . में दर्ज करा सकता है ।

अथवा विधि व्यवसायिक के माध्यम से अपना मत प्रस्तुत करना चाहता है ।

और यह भी अनुभव करती है कि उस कार्य के लिये इसके साप संलग्न विवरणी में निर्धारित भूमि पर प्रयोक्ता का अधिकार 
ग्रहण करना आवश्यक है । 


अत : पैट्रोलियम एवं खनिज पाइप लाइन ( भूमि पर प्रयोक्ता का अधिकार ) ग्रहण अधिनियम , 1962 ( 1962 का 50 ) 
13 के उपखंड ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्र सरकार एतद्द्वारा उस पर प्रयोक्ता के अधिकार प्रहण करने 
की मंशा की घोषणा करती है । 

बशर्ते कि उक्त भूमि में अपनी रुचि रखने वाले कोई भी व्यक्ति अधिसूचना की तारीख से 21 दिन के भीतर शूमिल 
पाइपलाइन विछाने के विरोध में अपनी आपत्ति सक्षम प्राधिकारी, गैस अथारिटी ऑफ इंडिया लि ., ए 
योजना , विकासदीप बिल्डिग, 22-स्टेशन रोड , लखनऊ -226019, उ. प्र . में दर्ज करा सकता है । 

मौर ऐसी आपत्ति दर्ज कराते समय किसी भी व्यक्ति को यह विशेष रूप से निर्दिष्ट करना होगा कि वह पनि ने 
अथवा विधि व्यावसायिक के माध्यम से अपना मत प्रस्तुत करना चाहता है ।

का . प्रा . 647.. . -यत : केन्द्रीय सरकार को यह प्रतीत होता है कि लोकहित में यह प्रावश्यक है कि महाराष्ट्र राज्य , जिला 
रायगढ़ में मौजे बोरीस , तहसील अलिधाम से मौजे सालाब , तहसील मुरुड जंजिरा, जिला रायगढ़ तक नैसर्गिक गैस परिवहन के लिये 
पाईपलाईन गैम अथॉरिटी ऑफ इंडिया लि . , नयी दिल्ली 110066 के मार्फत बिछाई जानी चाहिए । 

और यतः यह प्रतीत होता है कि ऐसी लाइनों को बिछाने के प्रयोजन के लिये एतपाबद्ध अनुसूची में वर्णित भूमि में 
उपयोग का अधिकार अजित करना आवश्यक है । 

अत: अब पेट्रोलियम औ• खनिज पाईप लाइन ( भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का प्रर्जन ) अधिनियम , 1962 ( 1962 का 
50 ) की धारा 3 को उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का अधिकार अर्जित करने का अपना प्राशय घोषित किया है । । 

बशर्ते फि उक्त भूमि में हितबद्ध कोई व्यक्ति उस भूमि के नोचे पाईप लाईन बिछाने के लिये भाक्षेप सक्षम प्राधिकारी, 
गैस अथॉरिटी ऑफ इंडिया लि . प्रभू निजाम दूसरी मजल मु . पोस्ट , तहसील अलिबाग , जिला रायगढ़ महाराष्ट्र राज्य को इस अधि 
सुचना की तारीख से 21 दिनों के भीतर कर सकेगा । 

___ और ऐसा आक्षेप करने वाला हर गकिा विनिर्दिष्ट यह भी कथन करेगा कि यह चाहता है कि उसको मुनवाई व्यक्तिगत 
स्वरूप में हो या किसी विधि व्यवसायी के मार्फत ।

RAJIV MEHRISHI. Dirictor 
नई दिल्ली , 10 मार्च , 1993 
का . पा . 649.-~~~यत : केन्द्रीय सरकार को यह प्रतीत होता है कि लोकहित में यह प्रावश्यक है कि महाराष्ट्र राज्य , जिला 
रायगढ़ में मौजे बोरीस, तहसिल - अलिबाग मे मौजे सालाब, तहसिल मरुड जंजिरा, जिला रायगढ सक नगिक गैम परिवहन के लिये 
पाईप लाइन गैस अथॉरिटी ऑफ इंडिया लि . नई दिल्ली 110061 के मार्फत बिछाई जानी चाहिये । 

और यतः यह प्रतीत होता है कि ऐमो लाइनों को बिछाने के प्रयोजन के लिए तदपाबा अनुसूची में वणित भूमि में उपयोग 
का अधिकार अजित करना प्रावश्यक है । 


अतः अब पेट्रोलियम और खनिज पाईप लाईन ( भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का अर्जन ) अधिनियम 1962 ( 1962 का 
50 की धारा 3 को उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग अर्जित करने का प्राला प्राशय घोषित कर दिया है । 

___ बशर्ते कि उस भूमि में हिसबद कोई व्यक्ति उस भूमि के नीचे पाईप लाईन बिछाने के लिये प्राक्षेप सक्षम प्राधिकारी, गैस 
अथारिटी मांफ इंडिया लि . प्रभू निवास दूसरी मजल अलिबाग जिला रायगढ़ महाराष्ट्र राज्य को इस अधिसूचना की तारीख में 
21 दिनों के भीतर कर सकेगा । 


और सा प्राक्षेप करने वाला हर व्यक्ति विनिर्दिष्ट यह भी कथन करेगा कि यह चाहता है कि उसकी सुनवाई व्यक्तिगत 
स्वरूप में हो या किसी विधी व्यवसायी के मार्फत । 

नदी, नाला, बीज, क्रीक आदि का नोटिफिकेशन

का . डा . 649 - ~ यत : केन्द्रीय सरकार को यह प्रतीत होता है कि लोकहित में यह आवश्यक है कि महाराष्ट्र राज्य , जिला 
रायजगढ़ में मौजे योरीस , तहसील अलिफाग में मौजे मालाब , तहसील मुरुड जंजिरा, जिला रायगढ़ तक 
दिये पाईप लाईन गेम अथारिटी आफ इंडिया लि . , नई दिल्ली- 110066 के मार्फत बिछाई जानी चाहिये । 

और यतः यह प्रतीत होता है कि पेमी लाइनों को बिछाने के प्रयोजन के लिये एतद्पाबद्ध अनुसूची में वगित भमि में 
उपयोग का अधिकार अजित करना आवश्यक है । 

प्रतः अब पेट्रोलियम और खनिज पाईप लाईन ( भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का प्रर्जन ) अधिनियम, 1962 ( 1962 का 50 ) 
की धाग 3 की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए अधिकार अर्जित करने का अपना आशय घोषित कर 
दिया है । 

बर्ते कि उक्त भूमि से हितमद कोई व्यक्ति उस भूमि के नीचे पाईप लाईन बिछाने के लिये प्राक्षेप मक्षम प्राधिकारी, 
गैस अथारिटी आफ इंडिया लि ., प्रभु निवास सरी मंजिल अलिबाग, जिला रायगढ़, महाराष्ट्र राज्य को इम अधिसुचना की तारीख 
मे 21 दिनों के भीतर कर सकेगा । 


और ऐसा माक्षेप करने वाला हर व्यक्ति विनिर्दिष्टतः यह भी कथन करेगा कि वह चाहना है कि उसकी सुनवाई ठावित 
स्वरूप में हो या किमी विधि व्यवसायी के मार्फत ।

का . प्रा . 650.-- -- यतः पेट्रोलियम और खनिज पाईप लाईन ( भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का अर्जन ) अधिनियम , 1962 
( 1962 का 50 ) की धारा 3 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन भारत सरकार के पेट्रोलियम और केमिकल्स मन्त्रालय , प्राकृतिक गैस 
विभाग की अधिसूचना का . प्रा . 280- ई दिनांक 29- 5 -92 द्वारा भारत सरकार के उस अधिसूचना में संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट 
भूमियों के उपयोग के अधिकार का पाईप लाईन को बिछाने के प्रयोजन के लिये अपना प्राशय घोषित कर दिया था ; 

और यतः सक्षम प्राधिकारी उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 को उपधारा ( 1 ) के प्रधान सरकार को रिपोर्ट दे दी है ; 

और प्रागे यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उक्त रिपोर्ट पर विचार करने के पश्चात् इस अधिसूचना से संलग्न अनुगुणी में विनिर्दिष्ट 
भूमियों में उपयोग का अधिकार अजित करने का निश्चय किया है ; 

अब अत : उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त अधिकारों का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार 
एतदद्वारा घोषित करती है कि इस अधिसूचना में संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट उक्त भूमियों में उपयोग का अधिकार पाईप लाईन 
बिछाने के प्रयोजन के लिये एतदद्वारा अजित किया जाता है ; 

और आगे उस धारा 6 को उपधारा द्वारा प्रदत्त अधिकारों का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार निर्देश देती है कि उक्त 
भूमियों में उपयोग का अधिकार केन्द्रीय सरकार के निहित होने के बजाय गैस अथॉरिटी ऑफ इंडिया लिमिटेड , 16 भीकाजी कामा 
प्लस , रिंग रोड , मार के पुरम , नई दिल्ली-110066 में सभी बाधाओं के मक्त रूप में घोषणा के प्रकाशन की तारीख से निहित 
होगा ।

का . प्रा . 65 1.-.- पत: पेट्रोलियम और खनिज पाईप लाईन ( भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का प्रर्जन ) अधिनियम , 
1962 ( 1962 का 50 ) की धारा 3 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन भारत सरकार के पेट्रोलियम और केमीकल्स मंत्रालय , प्राकृतिक 
गैस विभाग की अधिसूचना का . आ . 381 - ड, दिनांक 29 - 5 - 199 : द्वारा भारत सरकार के उस अधिसूचना में संलग्न अनसूची में 
विनिर्दिष्ट भूमियों के उपयोग के अधिकार का पाईप लाईन को बिछाने के प्रयोजन के लिये अपना प्राशय घोषित कर दिया था ; 


और यतः सक्षम प्राधिकारी ने उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन मरकार को रिपोर्ट दे दी है ; 

और मागे यत: केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उक्त रिपोर्ट पर विचार करने के पश्चात् इस अधिसूचना से मंलग्न अनुमुधों में विनिविष्ट 
भूमियों में उपयोग का अधिकार अजित करने का निश्चय किया है ; 


अब अतः उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त अधिकारों का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार 
एतद्वारा घोषित करता है कि इस अधिसूचना से मंलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट उक्त भूमियों में उपयोग का अधिकार पाईप 
साइन बिछाने के प्रयोजन के लिये एतदमाग जित किया जाता है ; 

और मागे इस धारा 6 की उपधारा 4 द्वारा प्रवत्त अधिकारों का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार निर्देश देती है कि उक्त 
भूमियों में उपयोग का अधिकार केन्द्रीय सरकार में निहित होने के बजाय गैम अथारिटी प्राफ इंडिया लिमिटेड, 16 भीकाजी कामा 
प्लेस , मार के पुरम , रिंग रोड , नई दिल्ली 110066 के सभी बाधाओं से मुक्त रूप में घोषणा के प्रकाशन की तारीख से निहित होगा ।

The removal of Sri. R . Suresh , Developinent Officer from 
the service of the management of Life Insurance Corpora 
tion of India has led to this reference , 

2 . The management, Life Insurance Corporation of India 
initiated disciplinary proceedings against the workman , Sri. 
Suresh alleging five charges as per charge sheet dated 
4 - 5 - 1987 in connection with a proposal for Rs. 5 ,000 on 
the life of one Sri. Sasidbaran Nair, who had expircd about 
nine years ago on 13 - 9 - 1986 . lo pursuance to the charge 
memo the management ordered a domestic cuiquiry since 
thc workman failed to respond to the chargc sheet. Sri. 
M . Mukundan was the Enquiry Oficer who conducted the 
domestic enquiry in which the workman participated through 
out. The enquiry officer found the workman guilty of 
charges 1 to 4 . Accepting the findings of the enquiry officer 
the management ordered removal of the workman from 
their service .

cd are entitled ?

fluth . representatives till 9 - 2 - 93 . I may state here that on none

pears that neither the workmen nor their /his authorised re 
presentative (s) are interested in prosecuting the casc any more. 

3. Therefore, in vicw of the above. a no claim award is 
viven in the case .

held against Shivaramegouda -i valld and proper , The punish 
ment of dismissal is proportionate to the gravity of the 
mis-conduct. Shivaramegouda was dismissed w . e. f. 30 - 11- 88 . 
Appointment on compassionate grounds are made only when 
the emplovec dies while in service of the bank . In the Ins 
tant case Shivaramegouda died in the accident on 12 - 1 - 92 . 

7 . Evidence has not been recorded in case . The Learned 
coun : c for the party has filed the memo concedding that 
the DF. was fair and proper . He has filed another memo dt. 
17- 2 - 93 that the party does not challenge the perversity 
or otherwise of the findings of the report.

punishment 

on Shivarumegouda was dl propor 
tionate to 

gaitude of the misconduct. The order of 
dismissal o misconduct. The order of dismisal should 
be set astito 

9 . For the limited purpose of seeing whether the punist 
mert on Sbivaramegoud was adequate or not. I will look 
into the charge sheet. So fas as charge 1 is concerned it 
does not say who were the other close relativch of Shiva 
ramogonida who were partners of Ms. Manjunathi Enter 
prisca , So far as the II charge is concerned though it stated 
huge amounts were collected by Shivaramegouda it doch not 
mention event approximately the amount collccted by Shivul 
ramegovda . It does not mention tho names of the customers , 
except one Agarwal, from whom he collected amounts and 
cheated .

के संबंब मिाजकी और उनके कर्मकारी के बीच अनुबंध 
में निर्दिष्ट औद्योगिक विवाद में श्रम न्यायालय कोझीकोड के 
पंचाट को प्रकाशित करती है जी केन्द्रीय सरकार को

to be fait and lawful and lis fiuditt were not vitiated in any 
panner , that by itself watild not be a gtouto fot non - inter 
ference of the order of terminarlopi of service ” . Tbé Suptettic 
Court has been pleased to lay down in this yutgörity that 
the erring workman should be giveti an opportunity to reform 
himself and prove to be loyal and disciplined employee . 

11. It it abundantly clear from the material on record 
that the Bank has not suiffcred in any way from the alleg 
ed misconduct of Shivaramegouda . 

12. For the aforesaid reasons I am of opiniod that the 
order of dismissal passed by the Il party , clismissing Shivara 
mcgouda is disproportionate to the nature of magnitude of 
the misconduct committee hy him , If back wages are denied 
that itsolf will be a punishment . 

13 . For the aforesaid reasons I set aside the order of dig 
missal of Shivaramegouda . If he were alive , he was entitled 
to be reinstated, 

14 . The claimant is $ mt. Bhagya Laxmi, wife of lato 
Shivaramegouda . She has prayed that she should be appoint 
od in the II party Bank on compassionato grounds. The 
Learned counsel for the I party has produced zerox copy of 
the circular of Il party as per the provisions of which ap 
pointments on compassionate grounds are made. The learned 
counsel for the TI party did not dispute that I party has 
got power as per circular to make appointment on compas . 
gjonate grounds. It is argued by the learnod couns: 1 for the 

| party that this Tribunal canpot give a finding that the I 
party is entitled for appointment on compassionate grounds 
and at best this Tribunal can only direct thc ſi party to 
consider the case of I party for appointment on compag 
sionate grounds. This argument cannot be accepted since the 
reference clearly directs this Tribunal to give a firding whe 
ther or not the I party wifo of late Shivaramegouda , is en 
titled for appointment on compassionate grounds in the 
Bank. 

15. It has been laid down by the Gujarath High Court in 
1990 ( 1 ) L .LJ. 132 Divya Ben D Oza v /s . Divisional Cont 
roller, State Road Transport Corporation , Ahmedabad ) that 
policy to give appointment on compassionate grounds should 
he liberally construed . In the authority of the Supreme Court 
(Scootcr India Ltd ., vs. Labour Court, Lucknow ) referred 
to above, it has been laid down by the Supreme Court that 
Labour Court can temper justice with morcy. 

16 . It is averred in the counter statement that Shivarame 
gouda died as a result of the accident, subsequent to dis 
missal and so I party is not entitled to appointment. This 
argument doos not hold water . Once the order of dismissal 
is set aside the roinstatement ordered with continuity of ser 
vice , the employee must be deemed to be in service on the 
date of dismissal. 

17 . It is not disputed that the I party Smt. Bhagva Laxmi, 
wife of the deceased workman , has completed B . A ., degree 
course . It is stated in the claim petition that she has no 
means of maintenance and she has to give education to her 
two children and she is in extreme difficultios . Bearing this 
Aspect in mind, I pass the following order ; 

ORDER 
It is declared that the action of the management of II party 
in dismissing M . Shivaramegouda from service was not jus 
tifled . The I party Bhagya Laxmi is entitled for appointment 
on compassionate grounds in the bank , No backawges . Award 
passed accepting the reference as stated heroin . Submit to 
Government,

AWARD 
By the Order No , L - 12912 / 159 /91- IR . B . III tho dispute 
between the aforesaid parties in connection with the justi 
fiability of the imposition of a punishment of recovery of 
50 per cent of the loss of gold ornaments from the salary 
of a junior clerk by name Sri P . Krishnan was referred to 
this court for adjudication by the Central Government under 
Section 10 of the Industrial Disputes Act. 

2 . After the receipt of the reference order in this court , 
pursuant to the notice issuod from this court both the manage 
meat and the union entered appearance and filed claim state 
ments setting forth their stand in the dispute, The brief 
avernments in the statement filed by the union are as 
follows : - The worker P , Krishnan joined the service of the 
management bank as a Junior Clerk and he was working as 
such in its Chakkittupara Branch , Whilo so in August 1987 ho 
was put in charge of the Security locker in the bank along with 
the Manager and ho was ontrusted with tho socond koy of the 
locker. While so on 31st August, 1987, 41. 800 grams of gold 
pledged by one K . Raghayan , on 21st January , 1987 was 
found missing from tho locker . Though a police complaint 
was preferred by the management the missing articles cannot 
be traced out. But however just to fix tho liability on some 
body the worker was proceeded against by the management 
by issuing him a memo of charges stating that as he was in 
charge of the second key of the locker at the time of the 
detection of the theft he is accountable for tho loss . Though 
the worker has given a prompt reply pleading innocence and 
that he cannot be held liable , the management was not satis

fictd with that explanation and accordingly a domestic enquiry 
was conducted by the management in this mattor. This en 
quiry was in fact conducted violating all norms of natural 
justice as the worker way not given proper opportunity to de 
fend himself in that enquiry . Later the Enquiry Officer gave a 

4 . urt to the management holding the worker accountable for 
the loss and basing on that finding 50 per cent of the value 
of the said missing article was ordered to be recovered from 
ilie salary of the worker as punishment. This action of the 
management is illegal and unjust as there is absolutely no 
evidence to say that the gold was actually lost during the 
period when the worker way in charge of the second key . 
Ivíoreover the finding of the Enquiry Officer is absolutely per 
verse as it is not at all based on any evidence . Hence the 
action of the management directing the worker to pay 50 
per cent of thọ value of the gold is unsustainable, An award 
may therefore be passed setting aside this action of thc man 
agement. 

3 . The brief averments in the statement fied by the man 
agement are as follows : - It is true that the worker 
P . Krishnan who is employed as a worker in the manage 
ment bank was directed to pay 50 per cent of the value of 
gold which was found missing from the locker on 31st 
August, 1987, This order is passed as at the time of the 
detection of the theft he was in charge of the second key 
of the locker , the Manager being in charge of the first key. 
Thug as the seront key holdor it is the responsibility of the 
worker to kecp all the securities in the security locker in 
safe custody. In this connection it is pertinent to mention 
that for this duty he is paid extra allowance . Thus as the 
worker was in charge of the second kcy of the locker at the 
time of the detection of the theft he is accountable to the 
hank for the loss sustained to the bank . But however only 50 
per cent of its valko was ordered to be recovered from him 
and the balance 50 per cent was recovered from the Manager 
who is in charge of the first key . That apart the Manager 
was also given another punishment of cutting of increme nt . 
Although the fact that the worker was in charge of the second 
Ley at the time of the detection of the theft is admitted by 
the woiker still the management did not proceed against him 
straight away and he was asked to pay 50 per cent of its 
value only after conducting a domestic enquiry in which lie . 
was found guilty . The allegation that this enquiry was con 
ducted violating all norms of natural justice is absolutely 
falso . In fact the worker was given all opportunity to state 
his case and defend himself in that enquiry . Hence an award 
may be passei ratifying this action of the management. 

4 . On these pleadings the following points are formulated 
for decision : 
( 1) Whether the domestic enquiry conducted by the 

management is valid and legal ? 
( 2 ) Whethed the finding of the Enquiry Officer holding 

the worker accountable for the loss along with the 

Manager is sustainable ? 
( 3 ) What is the proper punishment if any to be awarded 

to the worker 


8 . Any way on fact there is absolutely no dispute be 
the parties. It is the admitled fact that 41. 800 grams of 
pledged by ono K . Raghavan on 21st January , 1987 
which was kept in the security locker was found missing 
31st August, 1987 when a physical verification of securi 
was done on 31st August, 1987. It is also the admitted ca . 
that from 17th August, 1987 to 7th September, 1987 the 
worker was the custodiun of the second key of the locker 
and the first key was with the Manager . It is also thoad 
mitted case that this locker can be opened by putting both the 
heys simultuneously and hence if either of the key is missing 
it cannot be opened . Now the stand of the management is 
that as the worker is admittedly the custodian of the second 
key at the time of the detection of the theft he is account 
able for the loss. But the grievance of the worker is that 
he cannot be lield liable since it is not the date of detection 
of the theft that is material but the date of actual theft. 
According to union as it was pledged on 21st January , 1987 
and in as much as it was found missing only on 31st August, 
1987 the theft can be any day in between 21st January , 1987 
und 31st August , 1987 and hence the worker who was put 
in charge of the second key only on 17th August, 1987 can 
not be held liable on the ground that at the time of the 
detection of the theft he was in charge of the second kcy 
since theft can be prior to 17th August, 1987 as well. This 
is exactly his stand before the Enquiry Officer also but the 
Enquiry Officer after evaluating the evidence produced before 
him by the management over-ruled this objection and found 
him accountable for the loss. Now let me see how far this 
finding is correct. 

9 . Any way I do not have to cvalualo the evidence in 
detail, sincc as stated earlier on facts there is absolutely no 
dispute between the parties. Or in other words as observed 
earlier the controversy is only when the date of actual theft 
iy not known whether the person who was in charge at the 
time of the detection of the theft can be proceeded against. 
Though the stand of the union that the worker cannot be 
held accou ntable unless and until it is proved that the theft 
actually occurred when he was in charge of the second key 
carries little bit of weight, it cannot be accepted sincc ad 
mittculy the worker was in charge of the second key from 
17th August, 1987 to 7th Septeinhed , 1987 . This is ſucher clear 
from Ext, M2 Key Register. Concededly this theft was de 
tected on 31st August, 1987 i.c . at a time when the worker 
was in charge of the sccond key . If really the theft is prior 
to 17th August, 1987 though detected only on 31st August, 
1987 as now allegedly by the union , the worker would have 
detected this on 17th August, 1987 itself when he was put 
in charge of the soconu key since as per rules he has to physi 
cally verify cach and every articles kept in the security locker 
hefore taking charge of the second key . Whether he has 
actually verified before taking charge of the second hcy is al 
different matter. If he has taken charge of the second kcy 
without actually verifying the lockor he has done it at his 
risk . Any way from this , one thing is obvious that the thieft 
was detected at a time when the worker was in charge of 
the second key namely after 17th August, 1987. If really it 
was prior to 17th August, 1987 the worker would have detect 
ed it when he was put in charge of the second key on 17th 
August, 1987. Thus I find clinching evidence to conclude 
that 41. 800 grams of gold was stolen from the locker at a 
time when the worker was in charge of the second key . Ac 
cordingly I confirm the finding of the Enquiry Officer hold 
ing him accountable for the loss. 

10 . Point No. 3. - Then comes the question of punishment. 
Strictly speaking it is not a punishment because the man 
agement has not taken any punitive action against the worker 
and all that the management has done is that the worker was 
asked to pay 50 rer ce nt of the gold thụ , lost in monthly 
instalments from his salary . Thus the management was very 
lenient to him although as the person in charge of the 
second key of the locker he is personally accountable for the 
loss along with the Manager . So much so this action of the 
management tlocs not call for any interfedence . 

11 . In the result an award is passed fully ratifying the 
action of the management. 

12 . This award will come into force 30 days after its publi 
cation in the official gazette , 

Dictated to the Confidential Assistant, transcribed by him , 
Tevised. corrected and passed by me on the 17th day of 
February , 1993 . 

K , G , GOPALAKRISHNAN , Presiding Officer 


(4 ) Result ? 


5 . The evidence consists of the oral testimony of MW1 
and the documents marked as Exts. M1 and M2. 


6 . Point No. 1 , — This point with regard to the validity of 
domestic enquiry was decided by me as a preliminary point 
by order dated 8th July , 1992 holding that the domestic 
cnquiry is valid and legal. 


7 . Point No. 2 , - As stated earlier the charge against the 
worker is that when the worker was in charge of the second 
key of the security locker , 41.800 grams of gold pledged by 
one K . Raghava n and which was kept in this locker was found 
missing on 11st August , 1987 . According to the management 
there are 2 keys for this locker one with the Manager and 
another with the worker and as it can be opened only with 
these 2 keyg, both the Manager and the worker are account 
able for the loss sustained to the bank cn this count and ac 
cordingly 50 per cent of the value of the lost gold was order 
ed to be paid by the worker and the other 50 per cent was 
ordered to be paid by the Manager . Now it is this action of 
the management asking the worker to pay 50 per cent of its 
Falue that is called in question in this reference by the union .

AWARD 
The Government of India in the Ministry of Labour in 
exercise of powers conferred upon it by clauzo (d ) of sub 
section ( 1 ) and sub -sctcion (2A ) of Section 10 of 1hc In 
dustrial Disputes Act, 1947 ( 14 of 1947), have refeired the 
following dispute for adjudication vide their Order No. 
L - 38012 / 1 / 90 -IR (Misc .) of 1990 : 
“ Whether the action of the management of M / s. E . C . 

Bose & Co . (Paradip ) (P ) Lid ., Al / P . O . Paradıp . 
Dist. Cuttack , Steyedores / Contractors in refusing 
employment to Shri S . M . Ainin , ex - superviyor 
w .e .f , 1- 2 - 83 is justified ? If not, to what clief 

is the workman entitled ? " 
2. Shorn of all details , the case of the workman is that 
during course of cmployment under the first party manage 
meat he met with an accident is a result he became par 
tially disabled . He approached the Comniissioner under the 
Workmen s Compcnsation Act and the Hon ble High Court 
of Orissa at different times and certain orders were passed , 
in his favour by the Commissioner as well as the Hon bl 
Court. He was ordered by the Commissioner to be taken 
back in service but it was not carried out. Again he filed 
& complaint in the Hon ble High Court which was revister 
a as Misc . Case No. 2 of 1984 where a direction was given 
that ho should approach the employer within one week 
from the date of judgement for reinstatement and if he was 
not reinstated he might seek relief according to law . There 
after he knocked at the door of the Commussioner inder 
the Workmen s Compensation Act in 1985 alleging that 
despite of his repeated approach he had been deniew of emp 
loyment. Evidence both oral and documentary were led 
in the proceeding and the Comissioner without deciding 
whether his allegation of refusal of cmployment is true or 
not ordered for payment of compensation of Rs. 5 , 000 . Being 
aggrieved by the said order he preferred an appcal to the 
Hon ble Court , while dismissing the appeal the Hon ble Court 
observed that if the workman not allowed to render ser 
vice and is not paid wages it shall be open to him to 
approach the appropriate authority for redress. In view 
of such circction he raised a dispute which was admitted to 
conciliation and the same having failed was referred to his 
Tribunal for adjudication . 

3 . The first padty -management challenging the maintain . 
ability of the reference has urged that tho workman though 
joined service on 11 - 7 - 88 but thereafter without any reason 
able excuse remained absent which wounts to abandon 
ment of service and in this view of the matter he is not 
entitled to any relief in the present case . 

4 . During hearing the management remained absent for 
which exparte evidence wa recorded . The workman in 
his evidence has deposed that after being denied of job and 
compensation duo to the accident which he mct in course of 
his employment, he approached from post to pillar and 
having failed in his attempt has taken the last resort by 
raising the present dispute . In his unchallenged testimony 
he has emphatically stated that he approached the manage 
ment to permit him to join his duty but thc hanc was 
turned - down. There having no reason to disbelieve his 
unchallenged evidenice I hold that denial of employment by 
the management is illegal and unjustified , 

5. In the result, the action of the management rinder re 
ference being not justified , the workman should be rein 
stated in sorvice with full back wages with cffect from 
1 - 2 -83 and the back wages be paid within three months 
from the date of publication of this Award . 
Dictated and corrected by me. 

. R . K . DASH , Presiding Officer
